That's all.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any difficulty concentrating or making decisions?\n\nPatient: I suppose. It's... yeah. Inventory used to just be automatic. I'll just freeze up trying to figure out what garnish needs restocking. Just... hard to think straight, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods in the last two months where you felt somewhat better for a few days?\n\nPatient: I don't think so. No. It's all just... been about the same, really. Every day.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're moving more slowly than you used to?\n\nPatient: No. I don't... I don't think so. It's... no. Like I just said, it's pretty much been the same this whole time. I haven't felt normal.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any significant changes in sleep patterns since you started feeling this way?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I guess. I mean... I'm tired when I lie down, but my head just keeps going over things. Every little mistake I made at work, mostly. And then... when I finally do get to sleep, I usually wake up around four or five. It's... no. Like I just said, it's pretty much been the same this whole time. I haven't felt normal.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any significant changes in sleep patterns since you started feeling this way?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I guess. I mean... I sit when I'm at home, but... no more than usual, I guess.
